## 2. Global Summary
- `topic`: "ARM Cortex-M7 Devices Generic User Guide for programming Cortex-M7 processor-based devices, covering processor model, memory model, exception/fault handling, instruction set, core peripherals, MPU/FPU/cache facilities, implementation options, revisions, and glossary."
- `module_scope`: "Cortex-M7 processor, Thumb instruction set, CMSIS intrinsics, NVIC, SCB, SysTick, optional MPU, optional FPU, cache maintenance, TCM/AHBP/AHBS/AXI access control, debug/trace components at a user-guide level."
- `key_chapters`: "Chapter 1 Introduction; Chapter 2 Cortex-M7 Processor; Chapter 3 Cortex-M7 Instruction Set; Chapter 4 Cortex-M7 Peripherals; Appendix A Cortex-M7 Options; Appendix B Revisions; Glossary."
- `key_terms`: "Cortex-M7, Thumb, CMSIS, NVIC, SCB, SysTick, MPU, FPU, TCM, cache, WIC, ITM, DWT, ETM-M7, CTI, PRIMASK, FAULTMASK, BASEPRI, CONTROL, EXC_RETURN, DMB, DSB, ISB, LDREX, STREX."
